Across all scenarios, the proposed method accurately identifies the governing dynamics with significantly fewer data samples than random sampling.

</details>


### [99] [Re-evaluating Confidence Remasking in Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12232)
*Stipe Frkovic,Metod Jazbec,Dan Zhang,Christian A. Naesseth,Ilija Bogunovic,Eric Nalisnick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新评估了后验置信度驱动的掩码扩散语言模型自纠正方法（以WINO为代表），发现在标准解码设置（短块长度）下该方法相比单纯置信度驱动的去掩码几乎无优势；在非贪心解码下虽能缓解随机性引入的错误，但加剧了多样性坍塌问题。结论是后验置信度驱动的重掩码方法的收益高度依赖具体设置，亟需更全面的评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型（dLLMs）中，一旦token被去掩码就无法被修正，导致模型易受早期采样错误的影响。现有工作尝试通过训练-free的后验方式引入自纠正（重掩码）能力，但其实际有效性尚缺乏系统评估。

Method: 以WINO为代表的代表性后验重掩码方法为研究对象，在标准解码设置（短块长度）和非贪心解码设置下，将其与单纯置信度驱动的去掩码方法进行对比实验。

Result: 1) 标准解码下，后验重掩码相比置信度去掩码几乎无提升；2) 非贪心解码下，后验重掩码可部分缓解随机性引入的错误，但会加剧置信度去掩码已存在的多样性坍塌问题。

Conclusion: 后验置信度驱动的重掩码方法的收益高度依赖于解码设置，现有评估框架不足以全面衡量其优劣，需要更系统的评估体系。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (dLLMs) have recently emerged as a competitive alternative to autoregressive language models, with the promise of faster inference via parallel token generation. A notable limitation of the masked formulation, however, is that once a token has been unmasked it can no longer be revised, leaving dLLMs vulnerable to early sampling mistakes. To address this, a growing body of work has sought to extend masked dLLMs with self-correcting (remasking) capabilities. One appealing subset of these methods does so in a training-free, post-hoc manner based on token confidences, with encouraging early reported results. In this work, we revisit the empirical evaluation of a representative post-hoc remasking method, WINO [Hong et al., 2026], and find that under standard decoding settings (shorter block lengths) it brings little-to-no benefit over confidence-based unmasking alone [Wu et al., 2025]. Extending the evaluation to non-greedy decoding, we find that while confidence-based remasking can mitigate errors introduced by increased stochasticity to some extent, it also exacerbates the diversity collapse previously reported for confidence-based unmasking. Overall, our results show that the benefits of post-hoc confidence-based remasking are highly setting-dependent, underscoring the need for a more comprehensive evaluation framework.

</details>


### [100] [Finding Multiple Interpretations in Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12277)
*Matthew Chak,Paul Anderson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种方法，用于寻找在损失/准确率方面表现相似但上下文感知特征高度不同的模型集。通过在METABRIC数据集上的实验，证明该方法能发现与对照组相比基因表达高度不同的多个模型，且无性能损失。该方法对于分析模型全局特征以提取对潜在现象的理解具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常只关注模型性能指标，忽略了在相似性能下模型可能具有不同的内部特征（如基因表达模式）。为了更深入地理解模型所学习的潜在现象，需要一种能够识别性能相似但特征不同的模型集合的方法。

Method: 提出一种用于发现性能相似（基于损失/准确率）但上下文感知特征高度不同的模型集合的方法。在METABRIC数据集上，通过对比实验，将提出方法与对照组方法进行比较，评估模型在基因表达特征上的差异性。

Result: 在METABRIC数据集上的实验表明，提出的方法能够找到多个在基因表达上与对照组方法所得模型高度不同的模型，且这些模型在性能（损失/准确率）上没有显著下降。

Conclusion: 该方法对于需要分析模型全局特征以提取对所研究潜在现象洞察的应用场景具有重要意义，提供了一个在保持性能的同时探索模型多样性的有效途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose an approach to finding sets of similar-performing models (in terms of loss/accuracy measurements) with highly different context-aware characteristics. Through experiments on the METABRIC dataset, we show that the proposed method finds multiple models with highly different gene expressions than those found by the control methodology without performance penalties. We argue that the proposed methodology is important whenever one aims to analyze any global characteristic of a model to extract insight into the underlying phenomenon being studied.

</details>


### [101] [Holding the FP8 Quality Ceiling at 8-Bit Weights and Activations: INT8 and GGUF Post-Training Quantization of Ideogram 4.0 for Consumer GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12280)
*Deep Gandhi,Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Ideogram 4.0（93亿参数的流匹配扩散Transformer）在缺乏FP8张量核心的Ampere RTX 3090 GPU上进行INT8 W8A8后训练量化，证明INT8可保持FP8质量水平，优于NF4，且GGUF Q4_K在质量-内存前沿上表现最优；同时通过消融实验识别出FFN下投影层是质量关键，并分析了8-bit量化的增益边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大文本到图像扩散Transformer的后训练量化虽能在消费级GPU上运行，但硬件特定的质量-效率权衡缺乏直接度量，尤其针对缺失FP8张量核心的Ampere架构GPU。

Method: 采用INT8 W8A8方案（per-channel权重、per-token动态激活、SmoothQuant、混合精度保护高脆弱层），对Ideogram 4.0（9.3B流匹配DiT，含双权重副本用于无分类器引导，Qwen3-VL-8B编码器）在RTX 3090上量化，并进行200提示词基准测试、成对同种子bootstrap置信区间评估、逐类别OCR分析及消融实验。

Result: INT8与FP8质量无显著差异（Pick和CLIP的95% CI包含零）；INT8优于NF4（CLIP提升+1.9，95% CI [1.21,2.64]不包含零）；GGUF Q4_K在同等磁盘大小下优于NF4且为质量-内存Pareto最优；消融表明FFN下投影层保护是质量关键；INT8权重体积与FP8相当，在Ampere上需融合INT8内核才能实现加速。

Conclusion: 在缺乏FP8张量核心的Ampere GPU上，INT8量化可达到FP8质量天花板，GGUF Q4_K在同等内存下表现最优；8-bit量化在Ampere上质量无损但需专用内核才能提速，且FFN下投影层的混合精度保护是关键质量杠杆。

Abstract: Post-training quantization lets large text-to-image diffusion transformers run on consumer GPUs, yet the hardware-specific trade-offs are seldom measured directly. We quantize Ideogram 4.0 - a 9.3B flow-matching diffusion transformer (DiT), shipped as two separate-weight copies of a single-stream 34-layer backbone for classifier-free guidance and conditioned by a Qwen3-VL-8B encoder - for Ampere RTX 3090 GPUs, which lack FP8 tensor cores. Our INT8 W8A8 recipe (per-channel weights, per-token dynamic activations, SmoothQuant, and mixed-precision protection of a small high-fragility layer set) holds the FP8 quality ceiling: on a 200-prompt benchmark the paired same-seed bootstrap CI for INT8-FP8 includes zero on both Pick and CLIP, while INT8 improves on NF4 by $+1.9$ CLIP (95% CI $[+1.21,+2.64]$, excluding zero). A per-category OCR analysis, to our knowledge unreported for this model class, confirms text legibility is preserved, and an ablation isolates protection of the FFN down-projections as the dominant quality lever. Our GGUF Q4_K quantization beats NF4 at equal on-disk size and is the Pareto winner on the quality-memory frontier, with paired confidence intervals excluding zero (Q8_0 is quality neutral). Finally, we characterize where 8-bit quantization helps and where it does not: INT8's weights match FP8's footprint rather than shrink it, so a speed gain on Ampere awaits a fused INT8 kernel.

</details>


### [102] [Fourier Features Let Agents Learn High Precision Policies with Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12334)
*Balázs Gyenes,Emiliyan Gospodinov,Jan Frieling,Enrico Krohmer,Nicolas Schreiber,Xiaogang Jia,Niklas Freymuth,Gerhard Neumann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将点云从笛卡尔空间映射到高维傅里叶空间，以缓解神经网络的频谱偏差问题，从而提升基于点云的模仿学习策略在精细操作任务中的几何细节利用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于点云的策略在不同操作任务上表现差异显著，作者推测这是由于神经网络存在频谱偏差（倾向于学习低频函数），而基于笛卡尔坐标的策略架构尤其受此影响，导致难以捕捉几何细节。

Method: 将点云从笛卡尔空间映射到高维傅里叶空间，为点云编码器提供直接访问高频特征的能力，使策略能更有效地利用几何细节。

Result: 在RoboCasa和ManiSkill3基准测试以及真实机器人平台上，傅里叶特征在多种编码器架构和超参数设置下均显著优于笛卡尔特征，且表现出良好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 傅里叶特征能够比笛卡尔特征更有效地利用几何细节，有望成为基于点云的模仿学习的通用工具。

Abstract: High-precision robotic manipulation requires fine-grained spatial reasoning that is often difficult to achieve with RGB-only policies due to depth ambiguity and perspective scale issues. Policies that leverage 3D information directly, such as those based on point clouds, offer a stronger geometric prior over purely image-based ones, yet their performance remains highly task-dependent. We hypothesize that this discrepancy may be due to the spectral bias of neural networks towards learning low frequency functions, which especially affects architectures conditioned on slow-moving Cartesian features. We thus propose to map point clouds from Cartesian space into high-dimensional Fourier space, effectively equipping the point cloud encoder with direct access to high-frequency features. We experimentally validate the use of Fourier features on challenging manipulation tasks from the RoboCasa and ManiSkill3 benchmarks and on a real robot setup. Despite their simplicity, we find that Fourier features provide significant benefits across diverse encoder architectures and benchmarks and are robust across hyperparameters. Our results indicate that Fourier features let policies leverage geometric details more effectively than Cartesian features, showing their potential as a general-purpose tool for point cloud-based imitation learning. We provide source code and videos on our project page: https://fourier-il.github.io/fourier-il

</details>


### [103] [Claw-SWE-Bench: A Benchmark for Evaluating OpenClaw-style Agent Harnesses on Coding Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12344)
*Mengyu Zheng,Kai Han,Boxun Li,Haiyang Xu,Yuchuan Tian,Wei He,Hang Zhou,Jianyuan Guo,Hailin Hu,Lin Ma,Chao Xu,Guohao Dai,Lixue Xia,Yunchao Wei,Yunhe Wang,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Claw-SWE-Bench，一个多语言SWE-bench风格基准测试和适配器协议，用于在公平设置下比较异构的通用智能体（如OpenClaw）的编码能力。通过350个跨8种语言43个仓库的GitHub问题修复实例，实验表明适配器设计对编码性能至关重要（从19.1%提升至73.4%的Pass@1），且模型选择和智能体框架选择对性能影响相当（分别为29.4pp和27.4pp）。该基准将智能体框架和成本核算作为编码智能体评估的一等考量，并提供了完整和轻量级参考集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用智能体（如OpenClaw）作为自主工具使用者日益普及，但它们在SWE-bench标准下难以衡量其编码能力，因为通用智能体本身不满足SWE-bench所需的干净Docker工作区、补丁和预测合约要求。

Method: 设计了Claw-SWE-Bench基准测试和适配器协议，通过固定提示、运行时预算、工作区合约、补丁提取过程和评估器，使异构智能体框架在公平条件下可比；包含350个实例（8种语言43个仓库），并采用成本感知和排名感知的方法选择80个实例构成轻量级子集。

Result: OpenClaw在最小适配器下仅19.1% Pass@1，完整适配器达到73.4%（相同GLM 5.1骨干）；模型选择改变Pass@1达29.4个百分点，智能体框架选择改变27.4个百分点；精度相似的系统在API总成本上差异显著。

Conclusion: Claw-SWE-Bench将智能体框架和成本核算作为SWE风格编码智能体评估的一等考量维度，提供了完整的基准测试和低成本参考集，用于可重复的比较。

Abstract: General-purpose agents such as OpenClaw are increasingly used as autonomous tool users, but their coding ability is difficult to measure under SWE-bench: a generic agent does not by itself satisfy the clean Docker workspace, patch, and prediction contract required for scoring. We introduce Claw-SWE-Bench, a multilingual SWE-bench-style benchmark and adapter protocol that makes heterogeneous agent harnesses, or claws, comparable under fair settings including a fixed prompt, runtime budget, workspace contract, patch extraction procedure, and evaluator. The full benchmark contains 350 GitHub issue-resolution instances across 8 languages and 43 repositories, drawn from SWE-bench-Multilingual and SWE-bench-Verified-Mini after future-commit cleanup. We also release Claw-SWE-Bench Lite for faster validation, which is an 80-instance subset selected by a cost-aware, rank-aware procedure over 17 calibration columns. On the full benchmark, OpenClaw with a minimal direct-diff adapter scores only $19.1\%$ Pass@1, whereas the full adapter reaches $73.4\%$ with the same GLM 5.1 backbone, showing that adapter design is essential for enabling OpenClaw-style harnesses to perform coding tasks effectively. Across an OpenClaw $\times$ nine-model sweep and a five-claw $\times$ two-model sweep, model choice changes Pass@1 by $29.4$ pp and harness choice by $27.4$ pp under fixed models; systems with similar accuracy can differ substantially in total API cost. Claw-SWE-Bench therefore treats harness and cost accounting as first-class axes of SWE-style coding-agent evaluation, providing both a full benchmark and a low-cost reference set for reproducible comparison. The data is available at https://github.com/opensquilla/claw-swe-bench and https://huggingface.co/datasets/TokenRhythm/Claw-SWE-Bench.

</details>


### [104] [Anatomy of Post-Training: Using Interpretability to Characterize Data and Shape the Learning Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12360)
*Leon Bergen,Usha Bhalla,Sidharth Baskaran,Max Loeffler,Raphael Sarfati,Dhruvil Gala,Ryan Panwar,Santiago Aranguri,Thomas Fel,Atticus Geiger,Matthew Kowal,Siddharth Boppana,Daniel Balsam,Owen Lewis,Jack Merullo,Thomas McGrath,Ekdeep Singh Lubana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的后训练流程，利用可解释性协议分析偏好数据集中的潜在概念，使模型能够学习明确的行为特征而非模糊的标量奖励，从而避免学习虚假相关性（如过度风格化和谄媚行为）。实验表明该方法能诊断并缓解不良信号，同时增强安全性和模型个性等理想属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型后训练阶段主要依赖标量奖励优化，这种方式使从业者无法了解数据实际教会了模型什么，导致模型学习到虚假相关性并产生不良行为（如过度风格化和谄媚）。作者旨在解决这一问题，提出在优化前就能检查偏好数据集，并在概念层面决定模型应被允许学习哪些行为。

Method: 引入一个数据中心的後训练流程：使用可解释性协议开发统计假设，识别偏好数据中区分优选与次优生成的潜在概念，将其显式化用于细粒度用户反馈。统一了多种基于可解释性的训练协议，将其视为通过特征或数据干预来塑造奖励的方式。

Result: 实验表明该流程能够诊断现有偏好数据中的不良信号，减轻偏离目标的学习，并有助于放大或塑造期望的属性（如安全性和模型个性）。

Conclusion: 可解释性能够将后训练从优化不透明的代理奖励转变为审计和塑造学习信号本身的过程。

Abstract: Language-model post-training is the main stage at which model behavior is shaped, yet it still largely involves optimization of scalar rewards that summarize diverse desiderata. This abstraction gives practitioners little visibility into what their data actually teaches models, allowing spurious correlations to be learned by a model and inducing undesirable behaviors such as over-stylization and sycophancy. To address this problem, we ask: can we inspect a preference dataset before optimization and decide, at the level of concepts, which behaviors a model should be allowed to learn? Motivated by this, we introduce a data-centric post-training pipeline that uses interpretability protocols to develop statistical hypotheses for the latent concepts separating preferred from dispreferred generations, making them explicit for fine-grained user feedback. Building on this view, we unify several interpretability-based training protocols as ways of shaping rewards via feature or data interventions. Empirically, we show that our pipeline diagnoses undesirable signals in existing preference data, mitigates off-target learning, and can also help amplify or shape desired properties such as safeguards and model personality. More broadly, our results suggest that interpretability can turn post-training from optimizing opaque proxy rewards into a process of auditing and sculpting the learning signal itself.

</details>


### [105] [On Subquadratic Architectures: From Applications to Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12364)
*Anamaria-Roberta Hartl,Levente Zólyomi,David Stap,Pieter-Jan Hoedt,Niklas Schmidinger,Lukas Hauzenberger,Sebastian Böck,Günter Klambauer,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种次二次架构（xLSTM、Mamba-2、Gated DeltaNet）在代码模型预训练、大模型蒸馏和时间序列基础模型预训练等复杂任务上的表现，发现xLSTM整体性能最优，其优势源于更灵活稳定的门控机制带来的状态跟踪与记忆动态能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的二次注意力机制计算成本高，次二次架构虽能扩展但何种设计最有效尚不明确，因此需要系统比较不同次二次序列模型的性能。

Method: 在三种复杂依赖场景（代码模型预训练、大语言模型代码蒸馏、时间序列基础模型预训练）下，对xLSTM、Mamba-2和Gated DeltaNet进行对比评估；并提出统一公式化框架，从状态跟踪和记忆动态角度分析架构机制。

Result: xLSTM在所有三个任务上取得最佳整体性能；其门控方案能实现更灵活稳定的记忆校正；在合成长度泛化任务上验证了上述发现。

Conclusion: xLSTM在复杂任务上的优势源于其稳健的状态跟踪与累积能力，这得益于其门控机制的设计。

Abstract: Transformers dominate modern sequence modeling, but their quadratic attention incurs substantial computational cost. Subquadratic architectures offer a scalable alternative. However, it remains unclear which designs yield the most effective sequence models. We compare three leading approaches: xLSTM, Mamba-2, and Gated DeltaNet. We evaluate these models on tasks with complex dependencies: (1) code-model pre-training, (2) distillation of code models from large language models, and (3) pre-training of time-series foundation models. Across these settings, xLSTM delivers the strongest overall performance. To explain xLSTM's advantage, we present a unified formulation and analyze the underlying architectural mechanisms, focusing on state tracking and memory dynamics. Our results show that xLSTM enables more flexible and stable memory correction via its gating scheme. We corroborate these findings on controlled synthetic length-generalization tasks. Overall, our findings indicate that xLSTM's gains on complex tasks stem from robust state tracking and accumulation.

</details>


### [106] [Breaking Entropy Bounds: Accelerating RL Training via MTP with Rejection Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12370)
*Yucheng Li,Huiqiang Jiang,Yang Xu,Jianxin Yang,Yi Zhang,Yizhong Cao,Yuhao Shen,Fan Zhou,Rui Men,Jianwei Zhang,An Yang,Bowen Yu,Bo Zheng,Fei Huang,Junyang Lin,Dayiheng Liu,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对RL训练中rollout阶段的瓶颈问题，系统研究MTP在LLM后训练中的应用，发现MTP接受率受模型熵变限制，提出端到端TV损失函数和拒绝采样策略，在数学推理、代码生成和agent任务中实现高达1.8倍的异步RL训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL训练中rollout阶段是主要瓶颈，MTP虽可通过投机解码加速，但RL训练过程中MTP接受率显著下降，导致加速效果有限，亟需解决这一矛盾。

Method: 1) 揭示模型熵变与MTP接受率的负线性关系；2) 提出概率拒绝采样替代贪心草稿采样；3) 提出端到端TV损失函数直接优化多步拒绝采样接受率；4) 验证预RL阶段的MTP训练配合e2e TV loss和拒绝采样可保持全RL过程中的稳定接受率。

Result: 接受率提升约10%，最高达95%，推理吞吐量额外提升25%，在Qwen3.5/3.6/3.7模型上实现端到端1.8倍异步RL训练加速。

Conclusion: 通过系统分析MTP在RL训练中的退化机制，提出TV loss和拒绝采样等实用方案，有效解决了RL训练中MTP加速退化问题，无需在线更新MTP即可保持稳定加速效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a key component in modern large language models, yet the rollout stage remains the key bottleneck in RL training pipelines. Although Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) offers a natural solution to accelerate rollouts through speculative decoding, many studies have observed that MTP acceptance rates degrade significantly during RL training, leading to limited speedup performance. To address this bottleneck, we present Bebop, a systematic study of MTP in LLM post-training, and offer practical recipes to integrate MTP into large-scale RL pipelines. First, we reveal that the MTP acceptance rate is fundamentally bounded by the fluctuation of model entropy, which demonstrates a clear negative linear relationship with the rise of entropy in the RL stage. Second, we show that probabilistic rejection sampling largely alleviates the disturbance introduced by entropy in RL compared to greedy draft sampling. We further identify that the conventional MTP training objectives (cross-entropy or KL) are suboptimal in such settings, and therefore we propose a novel end-to-end TV loss that directly optimizes multi-step rejection sampling acceptance rate, yielding ~10% acceptance rate improvements, achieving up to 95% acceptance rates and up to 25% extra inference throughput gains across mathematical reasoning, code generation, and agentic tasks. Third, we test various online MTP training strategies during RL and show that pre-RL MTP training with e2e TV loss and rejection sampling achieves a consistent acceptance rate and speedup throughout the entire RL, eliminating the need for costly online MTP updating. We provide extensive experiments and analysis that validate our findings. Experimental results show our method achieves up to 1.8x end-to-end acceleration in async RL training of Qwen3.5, Qwen3.6, and Qwen3.7 models.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [TileFuse: A Fused Mixed-Precision Kernel Library for Efficient Quantized LLM Inference on AMD NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11357)
*Wesley Pang,Gregory Hyegang Jun,Feiyang Liu,Deming Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TileFuse是一个面向AMD XDNA2 NPU的混合精度内核库，通过协同设计权重布局、元数据放置、混合精度微内核和阵列级数据流，将AWQ风格的W4A16和W8A16量化格式直接部署到NPU上。相比全精度基线，GEMM性能提升最高121.6%，GEMV性能提升最高281%；端到端LLM推理中，预填充延迟降低2.0倍，能耗降低64.6%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘SoC集成的NPU在部署LLM时面临量化格式（如AWQ）与现有NPU软件栈不兼容的问题，专有软件栈对底层控制有限导致低比特量化难以直接映射，需要一种能够将现成量化格式直接部署到NPU上的解决方案。

Method: 提出TileFuse方法：（1）融合解包、反量化和GEMM/GEMV执行为单一内核流；（2）引入交错预分块布局支持高达32K的GEMM维度；（3）重新设计GEMV数据流以充分利用4x8 AIE阵列；（4）协同设计权重布局、元数据放置、混合精度微内核和阵列级数据流。

Result: GEMM性能较全精度基线提升121.6%，GEMV提升281%；较iGPU基线在GEMM上实现2倍以上性能和能效提升。端到端LLM实验中，预填充延迟降低2.0倍，能耗降低64.6%以上。

Conclusion: XDNA2是AWQ风格边缘LLM推理的实用目标，原生NPU对现成量化格式的支持可大幅提升NPU在客户端部署中的可用性。

Abstract: With the growing demand for on-device LLM inference, edge SoCs increasingly integrate NPUs to improve performance and energy efficiency under tight power and thermal budgets. However, practical LLM deployment on current client NPUs remains difficult: widely used quantization formats such as AWQ do not map cleanly onto many existing NPU software stacks, which are often proprietary and expose limited low-level control. In this work, we present \textit{TileFuse}, a close-to-metal mixed-precision kernel library for AMD XDNA2 NPUs that targets transformer linear layers in quantized LLM inference. TileFuse brings practical low-bit formats such as AWQ-style W4A16 and W8A16 directly onto XDNA2, rather than forcing the model to be reshaped around an NPU-specific quantization scheme.
  TileFuse co-designs weight layout, metadata placement, mixed-precision microkernels, and array-level dataflow. Specifically, it fuses unpacking, dequantization, and GEMM/GEMV execution into a single kernel flow, introduces an interleaved pre-tiling layout that supports GEMM dimensions up to 32K, and redesigns GEMV dataflow to utilize the full 4x8 AIE array. Across kernel-level evaluations, TileFuse improves performance by up to 121.6% for GEMM and 281% for GEMV over full-precision baselines, while delivering more than 2x performance and energy-efficiency gains over strong iGPU baselines on GEMM. In end-to-end LLM experiments on Ryzen AI laptops, TileFuse achieves up to 2.0x lower prefilling latency with more than 64.6% lower energy consumption. Together, these results show that XDNA2 is a practical target for AWQ-style edge LLM inference and that native NPU support for off-the-shelf quantization can make NPUs substantially more usable in real client deployments.

</details>


### [108] [Beyond Per-Token Pricing: A Concurrency-Aware Methodology for LLM Infrastructure Cost Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11690)
*Chitral Patil*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 现有LLM成本计算器普遍将GPU利用率作为固定输入（用户设定或默认为100%），但本文证明这是主导误差来源。在相同H100硬件上，有效成本从每百万输出token 0.21美元到15.25美元不等，低负载下低估代价高达36.3倍。作者提出以请求到达率λ为关键参数的测量方法C_eff = f(H, M, Q, λ, L)，通过42个基准测试验证，并发布vllm-cost-meter工具。同时发现FP8量化对MoE架构提升约2.2-2.4倍于稠密模型，且活跃参数数量而非总模型大小是饱和经济学的主要预测因子。在A100上的56次重复实验验证了负载驱动差异的鲁棒性（7.0-11.4倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源LLM成本计算器都将GPU利用率作为固定输入（用户预设或默认100%），从不基于运营商实际负载进行测量。这一假设导致自托管成本被系统性地错误定价，尤其对低流量工作负载严重低估实际成本。

Method: 提出测量方法论C_eff = f(H, M, Q, λ, L)，其中λ（请求到达率）是关键操作者可控变量，通过Little定律设置并发数。在H100上对稠密、超稀疏MoE和稀疏MoE三类模型进行42次基准测试，并在A100上重复56次实验。发布vllm-cost-meter开源工具，可附着于运行中的vLLM服务器实时报告实际成本。

Result: 在相同H100硬件上，有效成本范围0.21-15.25美元/百万输出token；低到中等负载下（1-10 rps）欠利用率惩罚为2.5-24倍，接近空闲时高达36.3倍。FP8量化使MoE架构吞吐量提升69-74%，稠密模型仅提升31%。A100上负载驱动差异为7.0-11.4倍。活跃参数数量而非总模型大小是饱和经济学的首要预测因子。

Conclusion: GPU利用率不应作为固定输入，而应基于实际负载动态测量。发布的vllm-cost-meter工具可使运营商基于自身流量获得真实的每百万token成本。FP8优势在无原生FP8张量核心的硬件上反转，框架已内置此硬件条件约束。

Abstract: Every public LLM cost calculator we surveyed treats GPU utilization as a fixed input -- entered by the user, baked in as a preset, or silently assumed at 100% -- never measured against the operator's actual load. We show that this assumption is the dominant source of error: on identical H100 hardware, effective cost spans \$0.21 to \$15.25 per million output tokens, an underutilization penalty of 2.5-24x across low-to-moderate enterprise loads (1-10 rps) and up to 36.3x near idle -- driven by one operator-controlled variable, offered request rate lambda, which sets in-flight concurrency via Little's Law and which no open-source calculator exposes. Because calculators take utilization as a user-supplied input, any utilization-naive estimate understates true cost by exactly 1/U, systematically mispricing self-hosting -- most severely over-selling it for low-traffic workloads. We propose a measurement methodology that parameterizes the relationship as C_eff = f(H, M, Q, lambda, L), validate it with 42 benchmarks across dense, ultra-sparse MoE, and sparse MoE models, and release vllm-cost-meter, an open-source cost meter that attaches to a live vLLM server and reports real \$/M-tokens against the operator's own traffic. We further show that FP8 quantization benefits the MoE architectures we tested roughly 2.2-2.4x more than the dense model (+69 to +74% vs. +31% peak throughput; n=3, broader validation needed), and our data are consistent with active parameter count, not total model size, being a primary predictor of saturation economics. To rule out single-hardware confounding we repeat the core sweep on A100 80GB PCIe (56 runs): the load-driven spread reproduces at 7.0-11.4x, the active-parameters ordering survives at FP8, and the dense-FP8 advantage inverts on silicon without native FP8 tensor cores -- a hardware-conditional caveat the framework already accommodates.

</details>


### [109] [Consensus Time in 3-Majority and 2-Choices Is Determined by the Maximum Initial Opinion Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11778)
*Niccolò D Archivio*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对完全图上的3-Majority和2-Choices共识协议，建立了以初始意见最大密度$||\alpha(0)||_\infty$为参数的紧确收敛时间界（强弱两种意义上），比以往依赖全局参数的结果更精确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作[Shimizu and Shiraga, PODC'25]给出了3-Majority和2-Choices在完全图上的上下界，但仅在弱意义上（对某些初始配置）匹配。作者希望在强意义上（对任意初始配置）获得直到对数因子的紧界，并揭示真正的控制参数。

Method: 对3-Majority和2-Choices两种动力学在同步模型完全图上进行理论分析，以初始意见频率向量的最大条目$||\alpha(0)||_\infty$为核心参数，推导共识时间的高概率上下界。

Result: 3-Majority达到共识的时间为$\Theta(\min\{||\alpha(0)||_\infty^{-1}, \sqrt{n}\})$轮（w.h.p.），2-Choices达到共识的时间为$\Theta(||\alpha(0)||_\infty^{-1})$轮（w.h.p.）。两个动力学的收敛时间均由局部参数$||\alpha(0)||_\infty$（最大初始意见密度）主导，而非全局参数（意见数k或$\ell_2$范数）。

Conclusion: 两个共识协议的收敛时间由初始意见的最大密度这一局部参数决定，而非全局参数。该结果在强意义上匹配到对数因子，比先前弱意义结果更精确。

Abstract: We establish the correct parameter governing the convergence time of the 3-Majority and 2-Choices dynamics on the complete graph in the synchronous model. Recent work [Shimizu and Shiraga, PODC'25] provides matching upper and lower bounds on the number of rounds to consensus, but only in a weak sense: the bounds are shown to coincide for some initial opinion configuration.  In contrast, we obtain tight bounds in a strong sense, with upper and lower bounds matching up to logarithmic factors for every initial configuration. Let $α$ (0) be the initial opinion-frequency vector, and denote by ___$α$ (0) ___ $\infty$ its maximum entry. We show that 3-Majority reaches consensus in $Θ$(min{___$α$ (0) ___ -1 $\infty$ , $\sqrt$ n}) rounds w.h.p., while 2-Choices reaches consensus in $Θ$(___$α$ (0) ___ -1 $\infty$ ) rounds w.h.p. Our results demonstrate that the convergence time of both dynamics is governed not by global parameters such as the number of opinions k or the squared ${\ell}$ 2 norm of the initial opinion distribution, but rather by the ''local'' parameter ___$α$ (0) ___ $\infty$ , the maximum initial opinion density.

</details>


### [110] [Optimizing Cloud Deployment: Blending of IaaS and FaaS for Microservice Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11824)
*Nikhil Kapoor,Sougata Mukherjea*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于指标驱动的方法，通过自动化框架分析服务级性能指标，将微服务从传统IaaS模型迁移至混合IaaS+FaaS模型，并使用两个微服务应用作为案例进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云计算的快速演进导致混合部署模式（IaaS+FaaS）被采用以优化资源利用、可扩展性和运营效率，但缺乏系统化的迁移方法论。

Method: 开发了一个自动化框架，通过分析服务级性能指标来识别最适合无服务器执行的微服务，并基于两个微服务应用进行案例研究。

Result: 揭示了不同云服务模型的优缺点，提供了一种可扩展且可复制的自动化方法，用于优化云原生应用的部署。

Conclusion: 指标驱动的迁移方法能够有效识别适合无服务器化的微服务，为混合云部署提供了系统化的优化路径。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of cloud computing has resulted in the adoption of hybrid deployments that blend Infrastructure-as-a-Service (IaaS) and Function-as-a-Service (FaaS) service models to optimize resource utilization, scalability, and operational efficiency. This paper presents a comprehensive study and practical implementation of a metrics-driven approach for migrating microservices from a traditional IaaS service model to a hybrid IaaS + FaaS model, using two microservice applications as case studies. The research develops an automated framework to analyze service-level performance metrics to identify microservices that are best suited for serverless execution. The findings of our research highlight the benefits and limitations of different cloud service models and provide a scalable and replicable automated methodology for optimized deployment of cloud-native applications.

</details>


### [111] [Harnessing Routing Foresight for Micro-step-level MoE load balancing in RL Post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11867)
*Yuming Zhou,Haoyang Li,Sheng Lin,Yanfeng Zhao,Tong Zhao,Xupeng Miao,Jie Jiang,Fangcheng Fu,Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ForeMoE是一个针对MoE强化学习后训练阶段的微步级负载均衡系统，利用可预测的路由信息分层规划专家迁移，在64 GPU上实现最高1.45倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有负载均衡系统仅适用于预训练阶段，依赖历史步级统计信息，无法应对强化学习后训练中微步级的高频负载波动。

Method: 利用RL多阶段流水线（rollout、recompute、policy update）中来自rollout阶段的可预见路由信息主动引导负载均衡；采用分层规划器将NP-hard负载均衡问题分解为可解子问题；设计传输引擎利用CPU辅助和GPU直连互补硬件路径实现专家迁移重叠。

Result: 在64 GPU上相比现有SOTA RL后训练系统实现最高1.45倍加速。

Conclusion: ForeMoE通过微步级粒度、前瞻性路由信息和分层优化，有效解决了MoE模型在RL后训练阶段的负载不均衡问题。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) and reinforcement learning (RL) post-training now dominate large language model (LLM) development, yet expert load imbalance remains a critical challenge. Existing load-balancing systems target pre-training by relying on historical step-level statistics. However, these methods fail under the unique workload dynamics of RL post-training: the step-level load is stable, but the tiny batch sizes processed during micro-steps cause severe, high-frequency load fluctuations.
  We introduce ForeMoE, a micro-step-level load balancing system for MoE RL post-training. Instead of relying on historical statistics, ForeMoE exploits the multi-stage RL pipeline (rollout, recompute, policy update) by using foreseeable routing information from the rollout stage to proactively guide load balancing in the remaining stages. To support frequent per-micro-step reconfiguration, ForeMoE employs a hierarchical planner that decomposes the NP-hard load balancing problem into tractable sub-components, alongside a transfer engine that leverages complementary hardware paths (CPU-assisted and GPU-direct) for overlapped expert transfer. Evaluations on 64 GPUs demonstrate that ForeMoE achieves up to a 1.45$\times$ speedup over state-of-the-art RL post-training systems.

</details>


### [112] [From Fork-Join to Asynchronous Tasks: Parallelizing Tiled Cholesky Decomposition with OpenMP and HPX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11937)
*Alexander Strack,Alexander Van Craen,Dirk Pflüger*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过Cholesky-Bench基准测试，比较了OpenMP和HPX两种运行时系统在分块Cholesky分解上的四种并行化变体，结果表明HPX在最优分块大小下比OpenMP快15%-30%，异步任务最多快26%，任务开销约小3.8倍，且去除冗余同步障碍可额外提升7%-14%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fork-join并行模型（如OpenMP）的隐式同步障碍对负载不均的算法性能有惩罚，而异步多任务（AMT）运行时虽能规避这些问题，但其相对于精心编写的fork-join基线的实际性能提升鲜有量化研究。

Method: 设计Cholesky-Bench基准，选取分块Cholesky分解这一不规则计算核，在GCC/LLVM的OpenMP实现和HPX AMT运行时上，实现并对比了经典fork-join、折叠fork-join（暴露更多内层循环并行性）、同步任务、异步任务（显式数据依赖）四种变体，在双路128核AMD Zen 2节点上测试多种分块大小和问题规模。

Result: 所有变体中，HPX在最优分块大小下比OpenMP快15%-30%；异步HPX任务比对应OpenMP任务快26%，任务开销小3.8倍；折叠fork-join变体接近同步任务性能；去除冗余同步障碍带来OpenMP 7%和HPX 14%的额外提升；GCC与LLVM在fork-join调度和任务创建开销上存在编译器差异。

Conclusion: AMT运行时（HPX）在不规则并行计算中相比OpenMP fork-join具有显著性能优势，任务开销更小；折叠fork-join和去除冗余同步是有效的优化手段；编译器选择也会影响最终性能。

Abstract: Fork-join parallelism, popularized by OpenMP, remains the dominant model for shared-memory parallel programming, but its implicit synchronization barriers can penalize algorithms with inhomogeneous workloads. Asynchronous many-task (AMT) runtimes sidestep these barriers by expressing work as a dependency graph of fine-grained tasks. Yet, the actual performance benefit over a carefully written fork-join baseline is rarely quantified. In this work, we introduce Cholesky-Bench and use it to revisit the tiled Cholesky decomposition, a canonical irregular kernel, comparing four parallelization variants of the right-looking algorithm across two runtimes: the OpenMP implementations shipped with GCC and LLVM, and the HPX AMT runtime. The variants span classical fork-join, a collapsed fork-join that exposes additional inner-loop parallelism, synchronous tasking, and asynchronous tasking with explicit data dependencies. We benchmark all eight combinations on a dual-socket 128-core AMD Zen 2 node across multiple tile sizes and problem sizes. Our results show that across all variants, HPX outperforms OpenMP at the optimal tile size by 15%-30%. Specifically, asynchronous HPX tasks are up to 26% faster than their OpenMP counterparts, and exhibit roughly 3.8x smaller task overhead. Furthermore, the collapsed fork-join variants close most of the gap to synchronous tasking. Removing redundant synchronization barriers yields an additional improvement of 7% (OpenMP) to 14% (HPX). A GCC-versus-LLVM comparison further reveals compiler-specific differences in fork-join scheduling and task-creation overheads.

</details>


### [113] [The PM-EdgeMap: Towards Real-Time Process Mining on the Edge-Cloud Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12103)
*Hendrik Reiter,Christian Imenkamp,Olaf Landsiedel,Andrea Maldonado,Patrick Rathje,Wilhelm Hasselbring*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在边缘计算环境下进行实时过程挖掘的可行性。首先提出了描述相关数据集和计算拓扑的形式化方法，然后通过基于边缘的合规性检查算法案例研究进行评估，结果表明边缘计算能够支持智能工厂中增强自主控制的实时过程挖掘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能工厂向CPS演进需要更高的自主性，这要求基于传感器数据的实时决策支持。过程挖掘可以提取洞察指导行动，但传统集中式计算难以满足实时性需求。边缘计算范式提供了一种潜在解决方案。

Method: 1) 提出描述相关数据集和计算拓扑的形式化方法；2) 通过基于边缘的合规性检查算法案例研究进行评估。

Result: 实验证明了基于边缘计算的实时过程挖掘在智能工厂环境下是可行的，并展示了其带来的益处。

Conclusion: 边缘计算能够有效支持实时过程挖掘，从而增强智能工厂的自主控制能力。

Abstract: Smart factories are evolving into Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS), demanding increased autonomy. This necessitates real-time decision making, facilitated by insights derived from sensor data. Process mining offers a valuable approach to gain such insights and guide actions. The edge computing paradigm supports this real-time requirement by enabling network communication between sensors and leveraging nearby computing resources. This paper investigates the implications of performing real-time process mining algorithms on the edge. Within this paper, we first propose a formalism to describe relevant datasets and the computing topology. We then evaluate the edge computing approach through a case study involving an edge-based conformance checking algorithm. The results demonstrate the feasibility and benefits of edge-based real-time process mining for enhanced autonomous control in smart factories.

</details>


### [114] [Efficient and Robust Online Learning to Rank in Decentralized Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12246)
*Marcel Gregoriadis,Martijn de Vos,Sayan Biswas,Anne-Marie Kermarrec,Johan Pouwelse*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出RankGuard，一种去中心化的在线学习排序（OLTR）框架，用户通过直接交换模型更新来协作训练排序模型。RankGuard通过基于用户私有点击历史（经位置偏差校正）评估传入模型来防御投毒攻击，仅聚合比当前本地模型更好地解释用户交互的模型。理论证明了收敛性，实验表明在四种攻击、四个基准和三种点击模型下，RankGuard在大多数设置中优于所有基线，且效率比最接近的竞争对手高62倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线学习排序系统依赖可信中央服务器收集和处理用户交互，但运营商可能引入损害用户利益的偏见。去中心化学习虽可避免中央权威，但恶意节点可通过投毒模型更新降低诚实节点的排序质量。因此需要一种能在去中心化OLTR中防御投毒攻击的鲁棒框架。

Method: RankGuard采用去中心化的方式让用户直接交换模型更新。防御机制的核心是：每个用户评估收到的模型时，将其与自己的私有点击历史（经位置偏差校正）进行比对，只有比当前本地模型更好解释历史交互的模型才被聚合。这使得恶意节点难以构造既通过测试又真正帮助用户的投毒更新。文章还提供了理论收敛性证明。

Result: 在四个标准基准和三种点击模型下，针对四种投毒攻击（包括一种强大的自适应攻击），RankGuard在大多数设置中优于所有基线，且效率比最接近的竞争对手高62倍。

Conclusion: RankGuard是首个形式化收敛性分析的去中心化OLTR算法，有效解决了去中心化场景下的投毒攻击问题，兼具强鲁棒性和高效率。

Abstract: In Online Learning to Rank (OLTR), ranking models are trained directly from live user interactions, but existing systems rely on a trusted central server to collect and process these interactions. This leaves operators free to introduce biases that conflict with user interests. Decentralized learning offers an attractive alternative, allowing users to collaboratively train a shared ranking model by exchanging model updates directly with one another, without any central authority. In such settings, however, malicious nodes can send poisoned model updates that degrade the ranking quality of honest nodes. We introduce RankGuard, a decentralized OLTR framework in which users collaboratively train ranking models and exchange model updates directly with other nodes. RankGuard defends against poisoning attacks by carefully evaluating incoming models against the user's own private click history, corrected for position bias. An incoming model is only aggregated if it better explains the user's past interactions than the current local model, making it fundamentally hard for malicious nodes to craft updates that pass this test without also genuinely helping the user. We derive a theoretical convergence guarantee of RankGuard. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first formal convergence analysis of a decentralized OLTR algorithm. We evaluate RankGuard against four poisoning attacks, including a powerful adaptive attack, using four standard benchmarks and three click models. RankGuard outperforms all baselines in most settings while being up to 62x more efficient than its closest competitors.

</details>


### [115] [Fair Comparison of Scheduling Algorithms on Heterogeneous Edge Clusters: A Continuous Adaptive Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12343)
*Zihang Wang,Boris Sedlak,Juan Luis Herrera,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向边缘-云连续体上AI工作负载调度的开源基准测试平台，统一了多模式调度（CMMS）问题下的控制器接口、负载驱动和SLO评估方法，通过424轮实验发现控制器性能排名高度依赖集群配置和工作负载，且分离原始SLO与稳态SLO能揭示切换成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CMMS算法评估难以公平比较，因为不同研究使用各自的控制器栈、单一工作负载，且未报告每次决策的开销。

Method: 构建开源基准平台，包含统一控制器接口、闭环负载驱动程序（支持多种模式）、双指标SLO评分（原始SLO和稳态SLO），在5种集群配置和2种负载强度下运行6种控制器（共424轮实验）。

Result: 控制器排名强烈依赖配置：深度强化学习控制器在轻负载下表现最佳，但在高负载下被基于规则的启发式方法超越近29个百分点，且每次决策开销高出约500倍；分离原始和稳态SLO能暴露切换成本，而单一聚合分数会混淆这些差异。

Conclusion: CMMS调度器评估必须考虑配置依赖性和决策开销，双指标SLO分解有助于更准确地理解调度器的实际性能。

Abstract: Modern Artificial Intelligence (AI) workloads deployed across the heterogeneous tiers of an edge--cloud continuum must satisfy multi-dimensional Service Level Objectives (SLOs) over latency, throughput, and output quality. For each incoming task, the scheduler picks both a target node and a processing mode (e.g., full or reduced inference precision). We call this class of problems \emph{Continuous Multi-Mode Scheduling} (CMMS). Comparing CMMS algorithms fairly is difficult because prior studies typically evaluate each controller in its own stack, under a single workload, and without reporting per-decision overhead. To close these gaps, we present an open source benchmark platform that features (i) a unified controller interface, (ii) a closed-loop workload driver covering multiple workload patterns, and (iii) dual-metric SLO scoring that reports raw SLO (overall compliance) and steady-state SLO (compliance during stable operation) separately. Running six controllers across five cluster configurations and two load regimes (424 episodes), we find that controller rankings are strongly configuration-dependent: a deep reinforcement-learning winner under light workloads loses to a rule-based heuristic by nearly 29 percentage points once load intensifies, at roughly 500$\times$ the per-decision operational overhead. We further show that separating raw from steady-state SLOs exposes switching costs that a single aggregate score would otherwise conflate.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [From Explicit Elements to Implicit Intent: A Predefined Library for Auditable Behavioral Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11207)
*Liu hung ming*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SemantiClean是一个模块化框架，从电商会话数据中提取结构化语义信号，通过共享元素库驱动可插拔推理目标（购买意图、客户分群、产品亲和力），优先保证可审计性、结构治理和sigma=0可重复性，而非边际预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统端到端预测器仅优化准确率，缺乏透明性和可审计性。作者希望建立一套具有元素级透明度和可辩护决策轨迹的框架，以牺牲边际预测增益换取结构治理和严格可重复性。

Method: 基于OSPI数据集，将24个行为元素组织为四层架构（功能层、交互层、系统层、上下文层），通过三种抗通胀机制（RedundancyGroup贡献上限、TieredPenaltyCalculator偏差惩罚、AdaptiveConstraintMode冷启动保护）保障信号质量。核心是LLM集成语义推理引擎，采用两阶段LLM驱动推理架构，在推理时利用完整元素元数据。

Result: 确定性引擎输出完全可重复（sigma=0）；LLM依赖的结果（E8, E10）在固定provider/model/temperature设置下存在受控输出变异性。性别推理目标在当前实现中不可用，已从所有定量结果中排除。

Conclusion: SemantiClean通过结构化语义信号提取和严格治理机制，在电商场景中实现了高透明度和可重复性的推理，尽管牺牲了部分预测精度，但提供了更可审计的决策支持。

Abstract: We present SemantiClean, a modular framework for extracting structured semantic signals from e-commerce session data and driving pluggable inference targets including purchase intent, customer segmentation, and product affinity through a shared element library. Unlike conventional end-to-end predictors that optimise solely for accuracy, SemantiClean prioritises auditability, structural governance, and sigma=0 reproducibility, explicitly trading marginal predictive gains for element-level transparency and defensible decision trails. Built upon the Online Shoppers Purchasing Intention (OSPI) dataset, the framework organises twenty-four behavioural elements into a four-layer architecture (Functional, Interaction, Systemic, Contextual) and enforces signal quality through three anti-inflation mechanisms: RedundancyGroup contribution caps, TieredPenaltyCalculator bias penalties, and AdaptiveConstraintMode cold-start protection.This report introduces the LLM-Integrated Semantic Inference Engine, a fully implemented two-phase LLM-driven inference architecture that leverages complete element metadata at inference time. All quantitative results reported herein are produced by this engine. Deterministic engine outputs remain fully reproducible (sigma=0); LLM-dependent results (E8, E10) are subject to controlled output variability under fixed provider/model/temperature settings. The gender inference target remains non-functional in the current implementation and is excluded from all quantitative results.

</details>


### [117] [Position: Hippocampal Explicit Memory Is the Cornerstone for AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11245)
*Sangjun Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张将显式记忆集成到大语言模型（LLM）中是通往通用人工智能（AGI）的关键一步，因为LLM的学习机制仅类似人类内隐记忆，而AGI所需的高级认知功能（如长期战略规划、元认知、符号推理）依赖于海马体支持的显式记忆，无法仅通过统计学习实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在各项任务中表现优异，推动了AGI的期待，但其底层学习机制存在根本局限——仅类似于人类内隐记忆，缺乏支持高级认知功能的显式记忆机制。作者基于神经科学发现提出这一观点，旨在推动显式记忆集成的研究。

Method: 本文以立场论文（position paper）形式展开论述，通过类比LLM学习机制与人类内隐记忆，借鉴神经科学中关于海马体显式记忆功能的发现，论证LLM需要显式记忆系统才能迈向AGI，并补充了人工显式记忆系统的计算需求。

Result: 提出了LLM学习机制与人类内隐记忆的高度类比性，明确了AGI所需的高级认知功能对显式记忆的依赖，并给出了人工显式记忆系统的计算需求框架。

Conclusion: 集成显式记忆是推动LLM迈向AGI的基石，仅靠内隐统计学习不足以实现长期战略规划、元认知和符号推理等高级认知功能。未来研究应聚焦于如何将显式记忆系统有效地整合到LLM架构中。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across various tasks, raising expectations for Artificial General Intelligence (AGI). This position paper argues that integrating explicit memory is the cornerstone for advancing LLMs toward AGI. The key reason is that the underlying learning mechanism of LLMs is highly analogous to human implicit memory. However, higher-order cognitive functions necessary for AGI, such as long-term strategic planning, metacognition, and symbolic reasoning, heavily rely on hippocampal explicit memory and cannot arise solely from implicit statistical learning. Drawing on findings from neuroscience, I advance this perspective and complement it with computational requirements for artificial explicit memory systems, hoping to foster further research and lay the groundwork for explicit memory integration.

</details>


### [118] [Can AI Agents Synthesize Scientific Conclusions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11337)
*Hayoung Jung,Pedro Viana Diniz,José Reinaldo Corrêa Roveda,Abner Fernandes da Silva,Haeun Jung,Enoch Tsai,Aleksandra Korolova,Manoel Horta Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciConBench基准（9.11K科学问题+专家结论）和SciConHarness清洁室评估框架，发现前沿AI agent在科学结论合成上事实F1仅0.337，且无约束评估存在数据泄露导致的性能高估问题，消费者级agent常生成不完整或矛盾的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学AI agent在高风险领域（如健康）合成结论的能力尚不明确，且缺乏可靠的评估方法，现有评估可能因数据泄露而高估模型能力。

Method: 构建SciConBench基准（9.11K问题+专家结论），提出SciConHarness清洁室评估框架（控制web交互防止泄露），采用原子事实分解和事实精确率/召回率评估正确性与完整性。

Result: 最佳agent在清洁室设置下事实F1仅0.337；无约束评估性能普遍高于清洁室设置，表明数据泄露导致高估；消费者级agent（如Google AI Overview、OpenEvidence）经常生成不完整甚至矛盾的结论。

Conclusion: 可靠的科学结论合成仍是一个开放挑战，清洁室评估对于评估开放域AI agent至关重要。

Abstract: Scientific AI agents increasingly retrieve evidence, reason across sources, and synthesize conclusions used in consequential decisions. Yet, their ability to do so in high-stakes domains such as health remains unclear. We introduce SciConBench, a large-scale live benchmark of 9.11K questions and expert-written conclusions from systematic reviews to evaluate open-domain scientific conclusion synthesis. The benchmark draws on an expert-validated automated evaluation pipeline that decomposes conclusions into atomic facts and measures correctness and comprehensiveness via factual precision and recall. To mitigate data leakage, we further introduce SciConHarness, a clean-room evaluation harness that equips agents with controlled web interaction to ensure valid measurement. Evaluating 8 frontier models and deep research agents, we find that factual quality remains low: under clean-room settings, the best agent achieves only a factual F1 of 0.337. Our clean-room setting consistently reduces performance relative to unconstrained evaluation, suggesting that leakage inflates estimates of models' true synthesis capabilities. Finally, we audit consumer-facing agents (e.g., Google AI Overview, OpenEvidence) and find they frequently generate incomplete and sometimes contradictory conclusions, even when the ground-truth answer is available. Overall, our results show that reliable synthesis of scientific conclusions remains an open challenge, and that clean-room evaluation is essential for assessing open-domain AI agents.

</details>


### [119] [Knowing When to Ask: Self-Gated Clarification for Hierarchical Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11349)
*Aijing Gao,Yiming Kang,Mengdie Flora Wang,Jae Oh Woo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ACTION-RATING框架，将信息寻求内化为智能体的动作空间，与导航动作在同一序数尺度上竞争。在30,000节点的HTS分类任务中，发现从强制性澄清到机会性澄清的机制转变，信息寻求有效性（ISE）从50%提升至74%，在受控答案通道下准确率提升达16.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次推理中失败常源于智能体在中间决策点因缺乏关键信息而进入错误分支。传统方法将澄清视为外部不确定性触发，而非智能体内在决策过程的一部分，导致帮助寻求行为不可观测。

Method: 提出ACTION-RATING公式，将澄清动作置于与导航动作共享的序数尺度动作空间内，使询问与行动在每个决策点直接竞争。定义两种信息寻求模式：强制性（无可行分支）和机会性（存在候选但残留不确定性）。采用信息寻求有效性（ISE）作为局部诊断指标。

Result: 在30,000节点HTS分类任务中，ISE从50%提升至74%。可分离性测试表明，即使答案质量下降（准确率-18.8%），信息寻求模式（模式拆分、ISE排名）仍保持稳定。受控答案通道下10-digit准确率提升+16.2%。

Conclusion: 将信息寻求内化到动作空间能够有效提升层次推理的中间决策质量，信息寻求的位置与所获帮助的质量具有经验可分离性，+16.2%的增益代表了更好定位可解锁的上限而非部署估计。

Abstract: In hierarchical reasoning, failures often originate at intermediate decision points where the agent commits to a wrong branch without recognizing that it lacks critical information. Rather than treating clarification as an external uncertainty trigger, we propose ACTION-RATING, a formulation that places it inside the agent's action space on a shared ordinal scale with navigation, so that asking competes directly with acting at every decision point and help-seeking becomes observable at intermediate states. Two structurally distinct information-seeking modes emerge from the agent's own ratings: mandatory (no viable branch) and opportunistic (residual uncertainty despite a leading candidate). On Harmonized Tariff Schedule classification (30,000-node taxonomy, three benchmarks, 9~LLMs across 4 families), we observe a regime shift from mandatory to opportunistic clarification, with Information-Seeking Effectiveness (ISE), a local diagnostic defined as the fraction of help interactions followed by a correct next navigation step (not a final-task metric), rising from 50% to 74%. Three diagnostic contrasts fail to reproduce this structure. A separability test shows that the information-seeking pattern (mode split, ISE ranking) persists when answer quality is degraded (-18.8% accuracy), supporting an empirical separation between where an agent seeks help and the quality of the help it receives. Under the controlled answer channel, accuracy gains reach +16.2% at 10-digit; we read this as an upper bound on what better localization could unlock, not a deployment estimate.

</details>


### [120] [Automated Mediator for Human Negotiation: Pre-Mediation via a Structured LLM Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11379)
*Jamie Bergen,Sarit Kraus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的结构化流水线自动调解员，用于替代人类调解员在谈判前的准备阶段（预调解）。该系统通过模块化分解（对话、偏好预测、批判性分析、结构化总结）实现，在受控人类实验中表现出与专业调解员相当的准备效果，偏好预测误差降低36%，并通过提示优化将过度肯定模式从36.6%降至16.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预调解对达成互利协议至关重要，但由于成本、时间和专业调解员资源有限，常常被省略。作者希望开发一种可扩展、低成本的自动化预调解方案，使更多谈判方能够受益于专业的准备支持。

Method: 构建了一个结构化的LLM流水线，包含对话模块、偏好预测模块、回应级批判模块和结构化总结模块。模块之间采用固定顺序前向传递，分离推理、生成和评估过程，以克服单一提示方法的局限。通过两次受控人类实验进行评估，与专业人类调解员进行对比。

Result: 1）在短期自我报告指标上（信任度、达成互利协议的信心等），自动调解员与人类调解员效果相当；2）偏好推理任务的RMSE降低36%；3）通过提示优化，过度肯定模式从36.6%降至16.8%，与人类调解员基线一致。

Conclusion: 结构化LLM流水线能够提供可扩展、低投入的预调解支持，在短期自我报告的准备效果上与人类调解员相当。系统的单方设计模式与人类调解员的实际工作方式一致，支持并行部署，具有较好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Pre-mediation, the preparatory phase preceding direct human negotiation, plays a critical role in achieving mutually beneficial agreements, yet is often omitted due to cost, time, and limited access to trained mediators. We introduce an automated mediator for human negotiation, implemented as a structured pipeline of LLM modules, that supports pre-mediation in integrative negotiation settings. The pipeline decomposes preparation into specialized modules for dialogue, preference prediction, response-level critique, and structured summarization, separating inference, generation, and evaluation to address limitations of monolithic single-prompt approaches. We use the term "agent" for each module following common LLM-systems terminology, but the components are not autonomous and do not interact peer-to-peer; outputs are passed forward in a fixed sequence. We evaluate the system in two controlled human-subject experiments comparing AI-based pre-mediation with professional human mediators in a multi-issue negotiation scenario. On short-term self-reported measures, the automated mediator achieves preparation outcomes broadly comparable to human mediators, including trust in the mediator and confidence in reaching mutually beneficial agreements, while achieving substantially lower error on the preference-inference task under our scenario and prompts (36% lower RMSE). A second study shows that targeted prompt refinements reduce excessive affirmation patterns from 36.6% to 16.8%, matching human mediator baselines. Our findings suggest that structured LLM pipelines can provide scalable, low-effort pre-mediation support broadly comparable to human mediators on short-term self-reported preparation outcomes. The pipeline's single-party design mirrors how human mediators run pre-mediation today and enables parallel deployment across all parties to a dispute, supporting scalability.

</details>


### [121] [INFRAMIND: Infrastructure-Aware Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11440)
*Ahasan Kabir,Jiaqi Xue,Mengxin Zheng,Qian Lou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: INFRAMIND是一个让多智能体LLM编排系统具备基础设施感知能力的框架，通过强化学习在共享GPU集群上动态调整规划、路由和调度策略，在高负载下实现高达99.9%的SLO合规率，同时将延迟降低7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在选择模型和拓扑时仅考虑任务和模型特征，忽略了共享GPU集群上运行时基础设施状态（如队列深度、KV-cache压力、延迟等），导致资源利用率低下：优先模型请求堆积，而同等能力的替代模型空闲无人使用。在多智能体流水线中，这种延迟还会沿下游步骤级联放大。

Method: 将多智能体编排建模为分层约束MDP，采用端到端强化学习求解。具体包含三个组件：(1) 基础设施感知规划器，根据实时系统负载和剩余预算动态选择拓扑和角色；(2) 基础设施感知执行器，观测每模型队列深度、缓存利用率和响应延迟，决定调用哪个模型及推理深度；(3) 预算感知调度器，重排序模型队列，优先处理紧急请求。

Result: 在五个基准上，低负载时相比基线准确率提升最多7.6个百分点，延迟降低最多7倍；高负载下基线合规率降至50%以下时，INFRAMIND仍维持99.9%的SLO合规率。

Conclusion: 将基础设施运行时状态纳入多智能体编排决策能显著提升系统在动态负载下的资源利用效率、延迟性能和SLA达标率，表明基础设施感知是多智能体LLM系统走向实际部署的关键设计原则。

Abstract: Existing multi-agent LLM orchestration methods, ranging from brute-force ensembles to learned routers, select models and topologies based on task and model features. However, these methods do not consider the runtime state of the serving infrastructure. On shared GPU clusters under concurrent load, this infrastructure blindness causes systematic resource underutilization: preferred models accumulate deep request queues while equally capable alternatives sit idle. In multi-agent pipelines, where each query triggers multiple sequential model calls, these delays then compound across every downstream step. Closing this gap is challenging because the relevant infrastructure signals (queue depths, KV-cache pressure, latencies) are dynamic and noisy, and they must drive three different decisions: planning, per-step routing, and scheduling. We introduce INFRAMIND, a framework that makes the entire multi-agent stack infrastructure-aware. An infra-aware planner conditions topology and role selection on real-time system load and remaining budget, biasing toward simpler graphs under congestion and richer ones at low load. An infra-aware executor then observes per-model queue depths, cache utilization, and response latencies at each agent step to decide which model to call and how deeply to reason; a budget-aware scheduler further reorders each model's queue so that urgent requests are served first. Cast as a hierarchical constrained MDP and solved end-to-end via reinforcement learning, the system learns to balance quality against latency automatically. Across five benchmarks, INFRAMIND delivers up to +7.6 pp accuracy over the prior baseline at low load with up to 7x lower latency, and sustains up to 99.9% SLO compliance under high load where every baseline drops below 50%.

</details>


### [122] [Forecasting Future Behavior as a Learning Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11445)
*Mosh Levy,Yoav Goldberg,Asa Cooper Stickland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将AI系统的行为预测视为可学习任务，通过训练行为预测器（Behavior Forecaster）基于推理轨迹直接预测LRM的未来行为，绕过了传统解释范式，在无需人工标注的数据上训练，性能优于GPT-5.4和Claude Opus-4.6。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRM）的传统解释方法难以泛化到长推理轨迹，且轨迹作为自然语言可读性差、忠实度低，导致难以通过解释预测模型行为。

Method: 将行为预测建模为可学习任务，训练行为预测器（Behavior Forecaster）以单次前向传播处理推理轨迹。通过查询LRM自动生成训练数据（无需人工标注），并对骨干网络进行端到端微调，初始化为目标LRM的参数。

Result: 在三个推理数据集上，Behavior Forecaster在预测答案重复概率和输入删减影响两项任务上，准确率超过GPT-5.4和Claude Opus-4.6的朴素阅读基线，且推理成本极低。端到端微调和从目标LRM初始化对性能至关重要。

Conclusion: 推理轨迹本身包含超越朴素阅读所能获取的关于LRM未来行为的信息，可学习的行为预测器是替代传统解释范式的有效途径。

Abstract: Trust in an AI system is often anchored by explanations of how it works, which one then uses to forecast its behavior on new inputs. For large reasoning models (LRMs), this conventional route is particularly difficult to follow: explanation methods for single token generations do not naturally generalize to long trajectories, and the trajectories themselves are often not faithful when read as natural language. We propose an alternative that bypasses the explanation step: treat behavior forecasting as a learnable task and train Behavior Forecasters that operates on a single reasoning trajectory to make the same forecasts one would typically seek from an explanation. The forecaster's training data is obtained by querying the LRM with no human annotation, and its inference is done in a single forward pass. We instantiate this approach on two tasks: how likely the LRM is to repeat its answer on re-runs, and how removing parts of the input changes its answer. We evaluate this approach on both tasks across three diverse reasoning datasets and find that trained Behavior Forecasters are more accurate than GPT-5.4 and Claude Opus-4.6 reading the same trajectories as naive readers, at a small fraction of their inference cost. We find that fine-tuning the backbone end-to-end and initializing it from the target LRM are each necessary for strong performance. These results show that the reasoning trajectory carries information about the LRM's future behavior that goes beyond what naive reading conveys.

</details>


### [123] [Search Discipline for Long-Horizon Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11522)
*Adithya Srinivasan,Devesh Paragiri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现，当科学有效性存在于异构区域/子群结构中时，基于聚合指标的自动化研究智能体会选择“全局分数高但局部结构崩溃”的错误候选者。作者提出一种外部控制循环协议，在智能体决策后基于细粒度证据进行审计，可降级或重审候选者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化研究智能体通常使用聚合指标（全局平均分数）来评估和选择科学候选者，但这种方法忽略了候选者在不同区域、子群或队列上的异质性表现，可能导致全局分数提升但局部结构被破坏的“逆向选择”问题。

Method: （1）证明聚合指标在存在异质性结构时会将错误候选者排第一；（2）在生态系统人口模型（Ecosystem Demography model）的火灾任务上进行实证演示；（3）提出外部控制循环协议：在智能体生成候选者后，由外部机制基于每个候选者的细粒度（分区域）行为进行审计，可降级智能体本应接受的候选者，也可重启智能体已宣布完成的任务。

Result: 在火灾模型任务中，全局分数最高的候选者与次优候选者分数在噪声范围内，但最高分候选者导致受保护的北方森林区域崩溃，而次优候选者则保留了这些区域。区分两者的关键不是全局分数，而是每个区域的局部表现。

Conclusion: 科学候选者的选择决策不应交由生成候选者的智能体本身（因为优化分数的智能体最不可能发现分数错误），而应转移到外部控制循环，基于可审查的候选者效应证据而非单一聚合分数进行决策。

Abstract: Autoresearch agents now propose, evaluate, and select scientific candidates against a metric, and that metric is usually an aggregate reduced over a heterogeneous space of regions, slices, or cohorts. We show that when scientific validity lives in that disaggregated structure, the aggregate can rank the wrong candidate first. The headline number improves while the structure underneath inverts, so a decision made on the number accepts a candidate that quietly breaks the model. The failure is not domain-specific. It appears wherever a candidate's validity is multi-dimensional but its verifier is a single reduction.
  We demonstrate the inversion on a fire-model task in the Ecosystem Demography model. The highest-scoring candidate and a slightly lower one are within noise of each other on global score, yet the top-scoring one collapses the protected boreal regions while the other preserves them. What separates them is the per-region behavior, not the headline number.
  This decision should not be left to the agent that produced the candidates. The agent optimizing the score is the last party likely to catch the score being wrong, and a prompt has no remaining turn once the agent has stopped. We move the decision to an external control loop that audits each candidate on its disaggregated behavior and acts after the agent has decided. It can demote a candidate the agent would have accepted, and it can reopen a run the agent had declared finished. Our contribution is the inversion finding itself, and a search-discipline protocol that decides on reviewable candidate-effect evidence instead of the score.

</details>


### [124] [MoCA-Agent: A Market-of-Claims Code Agent for Financial and Numerical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11537)
*Abdelrahman Abdallah,AbdelRahim A. Elmadany,Sameh Al Natour,Hasan Cavusoglu,Adam Jatowt,Muhammad Abdul-Mageed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MOCA-Agent，一种基于市场索赔机制（而非自由形式辩论）的代码智能体，将问题分解为原子索赔、通过交易者智能体买卖这些索赔、形成置信度加权的接受/拒绝决策，并生成可执行的Python程序，在十个金融与表格推理基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融和表格问答不仅需要流畅推理，答案必须精确基于事实、公式、单位、正负号和尺度。单个单元格误读或错误操作可能导致看似合理但实际错误的结果。现有方法在保证数值推理的精确鲁棒性方面存在不足。

Method: 将问题分解为类型化的原子索赔（claims），由专业交易者智能体买入或卖出这些索赔，通过市场清算机制生成置信度加权后的接受/拒绝决策，然后从市场支持的证据中综合出可执行的Python程序。代码感知验证器检查程序的执行、结构一致性及常见金融推理错误，最多允许一轮市场感知修复。

Result: 在十个公开基准上使用固定Qwen3.6-27B骨干模型取得强性能：FinQA 78.3%、FinanceMath 76.0%、MultiHiertt 71.2%、ESGenius 86.9%、FinChart-Bench平均85.6%。

Conclusion: 在原子索赔层面聚合证据，而非在整个答案层面，能提升高风险数值推理的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Financial and tabular question answering requires more than fluent reasoning: answers must be grounded in the exact facts, formulas, units, signs, and scales that support them. A single misread cell or incorrect operation can silently produce a plausible but wrong result. We introduce \textsc{MOCA-Agent}, a market-of-claims code agent that replaces free-form multi-agent debate with claim-level verification. The system decomposes each question into typed atomic claims, asks specialist trader agents to buy or sell those claims, clears their orders into confidence-weighted accept/reject decisions, and synthesizes an executable Python program from market-supported evidence. A code-aware verifier then checks the program for execution, structural consistency, and common financial reasoning errors, with at most one market-aware repair round. Across ten public benchmarks spanning financial numerical reasoning, general tabular reasoning, ESG question answering, and multimodal chart reasoning, \textsc{MOCA-Agent} achieves strong performance using a fixed Qwen3.6-27B backbone, including $78.3\%$ on FinQA, $76.0\%$ on FinanceMath, $71.2\%$ on MultiHiertt, $86.9\%$ on ESGenius, and $85.6\%$ average on FinChart-Bench. These results show that aggregating evidence at the level of atomic claims, rather than whole answers, improves robustness in high-stakes numerical reasoning.\footnote{The code and data are available: https://github.com/UBC-NLP/MoCA-Agent.

</details>


### [125] [SkillJuror: Measuring How Agent Skill Organization Changes Runtime Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11543)
*Zhiyu Chen,Zihan Guo,Bo Huang,Bingwei Lu,Jianghao Lin,Yuanjian Zhou,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillJuror框架，研究技能组织方式对LLM智能体的影响。通过渐进式披露（Progressive Disclosure）与扁平基线对比，发现技能组织方式能改变智能体的运行时行为（资源访问从1.18提升至3.85，有效采纳从1.33提升至3.92），并在410个匹配试验中带来17个额外通过试验（+4.1%），但收益依赖于任务类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准很少区分技能的内容与其组织方式，需要系统研究技能组织对智能体行为与表现的影响。

Method: 提出SkillJuror框架，通过语义受控变体、匹配多试验评估和轨迹证据，在固定任务知识的前提下，比较渐进式披露与扁平基线两种技能编写范式。

Result: 在82任务的SkillsBench研究中，渐进式披露将轨迹中不同技能资源的访问数从1.18提升至3.85，有效采纳事件从1.33提升至3.92；在410个匹配试验中，额外通过了17个试验（+4.1%）。收益具有任务依赖性：在需要指导实现、检查或修复的任务中有帮助，但在依赖精确输出格式、数值阈值或长流程生成的任务中效果较弱。

Conclusion: 技能组织方式不仅是呈现问题，它能改变智能体搜索和应用程序知识的方式，而结果收益取决于暴露的资源是否对任务可操作。

Abstract: Agent Skills augment large language model (LLM) agents with procedural knowledge at inference time, but current benchmarks rarely distinguish what a Skill says from how it is organized. We study this distinction through Progressive Disclosure, where a concise root file points agents to supporting resources on demand, and compare it with a normalized flat baseline. We present SkillJuror, a framework for evaluating Skill writing paradigms through semantically controlled variants, matched multi-trial evaluations, and trajectory evidence while holding task knowledge fixed. In an 82-task SkillsBench study, Progressive Disclosure changes runtime behavior before aggregate outcomes: distinct Skill resources touched per trajectory rise from 1.18 to 3.85, and effective uptake events rise from 1.33 to 3.92. It also yields 17 additional verifier-passing trials out of 410 matched trials (+4.1%) over the normalized flat baseline. The benefit is task-dependent. Progressive Disclosure helps when supporting resources guide implementation, checking, or repair, but is weaker when success hinges on exact output conventions, numerical thresholds, or long artifact-generation pipelines. These results show that Skill organization is not mere presentation: it can change how agents search and apply procedural knowledge, while outcome gains depend on whether the exposed resources are actionable for the task. Code is available at https://github.com/zhiyuchen-ai/skill-juror.

</details>


### [126] [HERO: Hindsight-Enhanced Reflection from Environment Observations for Agentic Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11559)
*Haoran Liu,Yuwei Zhang,Xiyao Li,Bohan Lyu,Jingbo Shang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HERO是一种事后增强的自蒸馏框架，通过将下一环境观察作为局部对齐反馈来解决多轮强化学习中信用分配问题，在TauBench和WebShop上优于环境反馈自蒸馏和GRPO，尤其在有限训练轮次预算下效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线自蒸馏方法在多轮设置中表现退化，原因是特权反馈（如成功轨迹或终端结果）与学生当前决策上下文之间存在对齐不足，导致信用分配困难。

Method: 提出HERO框架，在每个rollout完成后，利用下一环境观察作为局部对齐反馈，将观察转换为紧凑的轮次级诊断，捕获对原始动作的可操作反馈（必要性、有效性、失败原因）。

Result: 在TauBench和WebShop上，HERO在任务成功率和减少不必要轮次方面优于环境反馈自蒸馏和GRPO，在有限训练轮次预算下效果尤其突出。

Conclusion: HERO通过事后增强的轮次级诊断有效解决了多轮设置中特权反馈与决策上下文的对齐问题，在样本效率和多轮决策质量上取得显著提升。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning typically improves multi-turn agent capabilities through the terminal outcome of the trajectories, which makes it difficult to determine credit assignments for each intermediate turns. Recent on-policy self-distillation methods offer a promising alternative by converting privileged feedback into dense token-level supervision through a self-teacher. Our study is motivated by the unexpected performance degradation observed when naively extending this paradigm to multi-turn settings, which we attribute to a lack of alignment between privileged feedback, such as successful trajectories or terminal outcomes, and the student's current decision context. We introduce HERO, a hindsight-enhanced self-distillation framework that uses next environment observations as locally aligned feedback. After each rollout, HERO reflects on the completed interaction to convert each observation into a compact turn-level diagnosis, that captures actionable feedback about the original action such as its necessity, validity or failure cause. On TauBench and WebShop, HERO improves task success and reduces unnecessary turns over environment-feedback-only self-distillation and GRPO. It is especially effective under limited training turn budgets, where successful rollouts are rare and GRPO provides weak reward-contrast signals.

</details>


### [127] [Architecture-Aware Reinforcement Learning Makes Sliding-Window Attention Competitive in Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11634)
*Kai Liu,Peijie Dong,Xinchen Xie,Jianfei Gao,Qipeng Guo,Xiaowen Chu,Shaoting Zhang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SWARR方法，通过监督微调将预训练全注意力模型高效转换为滑动窗口注意力模型，再通过强化学习策略适应弥补性能差距，在数学推理任务上大幅缩小SWA与SA的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理和智能体LLM对长上下文推理需求增加，但自注意力计算量与上下文长度呈二次方增长，需要更高效的线性复杂度注意力机制，但SWA在数学推理中性能不如SA。

Method: 两阶段方法：(1) 从预训练SA模型高效转换为SWA并进行监督微调；(2) 使用强化学习进行策略适应，利用on-policy RL优化SWA约束下的自生成轨迹，缓解数据-架构不匹配问题。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，SWARR显著缩小了SWA与SA之间的性能差距，恢复了SWA转换过程中丢失的大部分准确率，同时保留了线性复杂度注意力的效率优势。

Conclusion: 强化学习改变了仅通过转换和监督微调对SWA在数学推理中可行性的结论，证明了RL是使SWA适配数学推理的关键环节。

Abstract: The rapid progress of reasoning and agentic large language models (LLMs) has increased the demand for long-context inference, but self-attention (SA) scales quadratically with context length.
  To address this, we study SWARR (Sliding-Window Attention with Reinforced Adaptation for Math Reasoning), a practical recipe for adapting SWA models to mathematical reasoning. SWARR has two stages: (1) efficient conversion from a pretrained SA model to SWA with supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which avoids pretraining a new base model, and (2) policy adaptation with reinforcement learning (RL).
  We find that SWA still underperforms SA after SFT, and we hypothesize that this gap is caused in part by a data-architecture mismatch: most SFT data are prepared for SA models and may contain long-range dependencies that are difficult for SWA to model. Because on-policy RL optimizes self-generated trajectories under the SWA constraint, it can adapt trajectories to better match SWA.
  Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that this recipe substantially narrows the gap between SWA and SA, recovering much of the accuracy lost during SWA conversion while preserving the efficiency benefits of linear-complexity attention. Our central contribution is the empirical finding that RL changes the conclusion one would draw from conversion and SFT alone about SWA's viability for math reasoning.

</details>


### [128] [TouchThinker: Scaling Tactile Commonsense Reasoning to the Open World with Large-scale Data and Action-aware Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11637)
*Kailin Lyu,Di Wu,Pengwei Zhang,Yuhang Zheng,Yingxin Lai,Long Xiao,Kangyi Wu,Pengna Li,Chen Gao,Lianyu Hu,Xiaobin Hu,Jie Hao,Ce Hao,Weihao Yuan,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TouchThinker是一个触觉-语言框架，通过构建百万级多源触觉推理数据集TouchThinker-1M（涵盖415个物体、8个场景、7种传感器类型）和动作感知建模机制，解决了触觉常识推理在数据规模和表示效率上的瓶颈，在多个数据集上达到与最先进模型相竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉推理数据集格式和规模有限，无法为从触觉观察到物理常识的推理提供充分监督，也难以学习可迁移的触觉常识；同时触觉信号固有的冗余性和动作特异性被现有方法忽视，导致表示效率低、语义表达能力有限。这两个瓶颈阻碍了触觉常识推理扩展到开放世界场景。

Method: (1) 构建TouchThinker-1M，一个百万规模、多源的触觉推理数据集，覆盖415个物体、8个场景和7种传感器类型；(2) 提出TouchThinker-Bench开放世界基准测试；(3) 提出动作感知建模机制（action-aware modeling mechanism）提升触觉表示效率，实现高效推理。

Result: TouchThinker在多个数据集上与最先进的模型相比取得了具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 从数据规模和表示效率两个角度出发，TouchThinker有效扩展了触觉常识推理到开放世界的能力，为触觉-语言系统提供了更扎实的数据基础和更高效的表示方法。

Abstract: Touch is a key modality for embodied agents to understand the physical world. Although recent work has incorporated tactile signals into language systems for tactile commonsense reasoning, scaling such systems to realistic open-world settings remains challenging due to two key bottlenecks: (1) current tactile reasoning datasets remain limited in format and scale, providing insufficient supervision for reasoning from tactile observations to physical commonsense and hindering the learning of transferable tactile commonsense; (2) Tactile signals are inherently redundant and action-specific, yet existing methods often overlook these properties, resulting in inefficient representations with limited semantic expressiveness. To address these limitations, we propose TouchThinker, a tactile-language framework that scales tactile commonsense reasoning to the open world from both data and representation perspectives. First, we construct TouchThinker-1M, a million-scale, multi-source tactile reasoning dataset covering \textbf{415} objects, \textbf{8} scenarios, and \textbf{7} sensor types, providing a solid data foundation for open-world generalization. We further introduce TouchThinker-Bench, an open-world benchmark with more realistic and diverse tasks. Then, we propose action-aware modeling mechanism to improve tactile representation efficiency and enable efficient reasoning. Experimental results demonstrate that TouchThinker achieves competitive performance against state-of-the-art models across multiple datasets. Our code and dataset will be made available at: https://github.com/lvkailin0118/TouchThinker.

</details>


### [129] [TreeSeeker: Tree-Structured Trial, Error, and Return in Deep Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11662)
*Zhuofan Shi,Mingzhe Ma,Lu Wang,Fangkai Yang,Pu Zhao,Yiming Guan,Youling Huang,Wei Zhang,Qingwei Lin,Dongmei Zhang,Saravan Rajmohan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TreeSeeker是一个推理时框架，通过分支-返回搜索和UCB信号控制深度搜索中的试探与利用，在多项基准测试中优于强开源基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索中，智能体面对多个看似合理的方向时，贪心跟随当前最佳方向可能陷入弱路径，而无纪律的探索会浪费预算。需要一种可控的试错机制来平衡探索与利用。

Method: 提出TreeSeeker框架，包含：(1) 树结构状态上的分支-返回搜索，每个分支对应一个子目标；(2) 基于UCB的信号（价值、不确定性、风险）来选择利用、探索或剪枝回退；(3) TreeMem模块将证据、不确定性、冲突等附着于所属分支，以指导后续决策。

Result: 在XBench-DeepSearch、BrowseComp和BrowseComp-ZH上，TreeSeeker一致优于强开源基线。

Conclusion: 显式的分支-返回控制机制能够补充更强的推理和工具执行能力，提升深度搜索的整体效果。

Abstract: Deep search requires agents to answer complex questions through multi-step web search, browsing, evidence comparison, and synthesis. A central challenge is deciding how to search when several directions look plausible but only some will later lead to reliable evidence. If an agent greedily follows the current best-looking direction, it may keep extending a weak continuation. If it explores without discipline, it may waste budget on disconnected trials. We propose TreeSeeker, an inference-time framework for controlled trial-and-error in deep search. TreeSeeker organizes search as branch-and-return search over tree-structured states, where each branch is a tentative direction for a sub-goal. At each round, TreeSearch reads all sub-goal trees, identifies active goals, and uses textual UCB signals of value, uncertainty, and risk to select among exploiting a promising branch, exploring an uncertain alternative, or pruning an unproductive continuation and returning to an earlier branch point. TreeMem supports this control loop by keeping evidence, uncertainty, conflicts, progress, and failure cues attached to the branches that produced them, so trial outcomes can guide later decisions. Experiments on XBench-DeepSearch, BrowseComp, and BrowseComp-ZH show that TreeSeeker consistently outperforms strong open-source baselines, suggesting that explicit branch-and-return control complements stronger reasoning and tool execution.

</details>


### [130] [Lung-R1: A Knowledge Graph-Guided LLM for Pulmonary Diagnostic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11675)
*Haoyang Zeng,Yuanxi Fu,Rongzhen Li,Yuming Yang,Xiao Sun,Jingwang Huang,Gujie Shao,Guohui Xiang,Quan Lu,Dongfan Ye,Xuetao Chen,Jiang Zhong,Kaiwen Wei,Zhi Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出肺科知识图谱LungKG（59,038节点，164,308边，15种实体类型和112种关系类型），并基于此构建Lung-R1肺科大语言模型，通过KG约束的推理链构建和KG引导的强化学习进行训练。在20系统评估中，Lung-R1-14B在选择题、肺科问答和EMR诊断任务上均达到最优，EMR诊断得分4.3583，超越最强基线0.1476分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在肺科知识问答和信息处理任务上有所进展，但可靠的肺科诊断需要对电子病历证据进行患者特异性、关系感知的推理，而非孤立的知识回忆。存在肺科知识与病例级诊断推理之间的鸿沟（Pulmonary Knowledge-to-Diagnosis Gap）。

Method: 1）构建LungKG——首个结构化肺科知识图谱；2）提出Lung-R1——基于LungKG引导的肺科大语言模型，通过KG约束的推理链构建和KG引导的强化学习进行训练。

Result: 在20系统评估中，Lung-R1-14B在Choice、Pulmonary-QA和EMR Diagnosis三项任务上均达到最优性能，EMR Diagnosis得分为4.3583，超越最强非Lung-R1基线0.1476分。

Conclusion: LungKG引导的训练方法对基于电子病历的肺科诊断具有显著价值。

Abstract: Diagnosing pulmonary diseases requires integrating heterogeneous evidence amid phenotypic variability and cross-disease overlap. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown progress on pulmonary knowledge question answering (QA) and information-processing tasks, reliable pulmonary diagnosis requires patient-specific, relation-aware reasoning over electronic medical record (EMR) evidence rather than isolated knowledge recall. We define this gap between pulmonary knowledge and case-level diagnostic reasoning as the Pulmonary Knowledge-to-Diagnosis Gap. To address it, we introduce LungKG, the first structured pulmonary knowledge graph for diagnostic knowledge organization and record-grounded reasoning. LungKG contains 59,038 nodes and 164,308 edges across 15 entity types and 112 relation types, serving as both a reusable pulmonary knowledge resource and the foundation for LungKG-guided model adaptation. Built on LungKG, we propose Lung-R1, a LungKG-guided pulmonary LLM trained through KG-constrained reasoning-chain construction and KG-guided reinforcement learning. In a 20-system evaluation, Lung-R1-14B achieves state-of-the-art performance across Choice, Pulmonary-QA, and EMR Diagnosis, reaching an EMR Diagnosis score of 4.3583 and surpassing the strongest non-Lung-R1 baseline by 0.1476 points. These results demonstrate the value of LungKG-guided training for EMR-based pulmonary diagnosis.

</details>


### [131] [Organize then Retrieve: Hierarchical Memory Navigation for Efficient Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11680)
*Hao-Lun Hsu,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Boyi Liu,Zhewei Yao,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HORMA是一种层次化组织与检索记忆智能体，通过文件系统式的层次结构管理经验，将摘要实体与原始轨迹链接，并利用强化学习训练的轻量级导航模块检索最小但充分的上下文，在ALFWorld、LoCoMo和LongMemEval等基准上以不到基线22.17%的token用量实现了更好的效率-性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体在处理长时程任务时存在固有无状态性问题，依赖不断增长的输入上下文会导致推理质量下降、推理成本增加和高延迟，亟需高效的工作记忆机制，而现有方法（有损压缩或基于相似度的检索）难以捕获多步智能体任务所需的时间结构和因果依赖。

Method: HORMA将工作记忆分解为两个阶段：1）结构化记忆构建模块，通过区分由信息缺失导致的失败和由上下文误导或过载导致的失败，迭代优化经验的组织方式；2）基于导航的检索模块，使用强化学习训练的轻量级智能体遍历层次结构，选择最小但充分的上下文。

Result: 在ALFWorld、LoCoMo和LongMemEval基准上，HORMA在受限上下文预算下提升了任务性能，在长对话任务中最多仅使用基线22.17%的token用量，持续实现更优的效率-性能权衡，并有效泛化到未见任务。

Conclusion: HORMA通过层次化组织与导航式检索机制，在保持详细原始信息的同时实现高效访问，相较于现有方法取得了更好的效率-性能平衡，且具备良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents struggle with long-horizon tasks due to their inherent statelessness, requiring all task-relevant information to be encoded in growing input contexts. The resulting degraded reasoning quality, increased inference cost, and higher latency necessitate efficient working memory mechanisms. However, existing approaches either rely on lossy compression or similarity-based retrieval, which often fail to capture temporal structure and causal dependencies required for multi-step agentic tasks. In this work, we present HORMA, a Hierarchical Organize-and-Retrieve Memory Agent that organizes experience into a file-system-like hierarchical structure, where summarized entities are linked to the corresponding raw trajectories, enabling efficient access without losing detailed information. HORMA decomposes working memory into two stages: structured memory construction and navigation-based retrieval. The construction module iteratively refines how experiences are structured by distinguishing between failures caused by missing information and those caused by misleading or overloaded context. The navigation module retrieves task-relevant context by traversing the hierarchy using a lightweight agent trained with reinforcement learning to select minimal yet sufficient context, thereby reducing latency along the critical execution path. Across ALFWorld, LoCoMo, and LongMemEval, HORMA improves task performance under constrained context budgets while requiring at most 22.17% of the baseline token usage in long conversation tasks. Compared to existing methods, it consistently achieves better efficiency-performance trade-offs and generalizes effectively to unseen tasks.

</details>


### [132] [Mind the Perspective: Let's Reason Recursively for Theory of Mind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11724)
*Chao Lei,Guang Hu,Meng Yang,Yanbei Jiang,Nir Lipovetzky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RecToM是一种推理时框架，通过递归视角构建来建模嵌套信念，将高阶信念问题简化为实际世界问题，在多个ToM基准测试上取得了SOTA性能，并在Hi-ToM上达到100%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提示的方法虽然通过可观察事件过滤或时间信念链改进了ToM推理，但未能显式建模嵌套信念，因此需要一种能够显式处理高阶信念推理的方法。

Method: RecToM通过递归视角构建（recursive perspective construction）建模嵌套信念：沿着问题指定的角色链，从上一角色的视角构建当前角色的视角，从而将高阶信念问题最终还原为最终构建视角下的实际世界问题。同时提供了KD45逻辑分析证明该视角构建形成了良构的信念模态。

Result: 在Hi-ToM、Big-ToM和FanToM基准测试上，使用多种LLM后端，RecToM一致优于近期先进方法，达到SOTA。特别地，在需要高阶ToM推理的Hi-ToM基准上，RecToM搭配GPT-5.4和Qwen3.5达到100%准确率。

Conclusion: RecToM通过递归视角构建有效解决了LLM在ToM推理中建模嵌套信念的难题，其KD45性质保证了信念模态的良构性，加上在多个基准上的SOTA表现，证明了该方法在Theory of Mind推理任务上的显著优势。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM) reasoning requires inferring agents' beliefs from partial and asymmetric observations, which remains an open challenge for LLMs. Existing prompting-based approaches improve ToM reasoning through observable-event filtering or temporal belief chains, without explicitly modeling nested beliefs. We introduce RecToM, an inference-time framework for ToM reasoning that models nested beliefs via recursive perspective construction. RecToM constructs each character perspective from the preceding character perspective along the character chain specified by the question, reducing higher-order belief questions to actual-world questions within the final constructed perspective. We further provide a KD45 analysis showing that RecToM's perspective construction induces a well-formed belief modality beyond simple event filtering. Experiments on ToM benchmarks, including Hi-ToM, Big-ToM, and FanToM, across multiple LLM backbones show that RecToM consistently outperforms recent advanced approaches, achieving state-of-the-art performance. Notably, RecToM reaches 100\% accuracy on Hi-ToM with GPT-5.4 and Qwen3.5, a benchmark requiring higher-order ToM reasoning.

</details>


### [133] [When Do Data-Driven Systems Exhibit the Capability to Infer?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11769)
*Maximilian Poretschkin,Tabea Naeven*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对《欧洲人工智能法案》中“推理能力”定义不明确的问题，基于统计学习理论构建了推理能力分级框架，并以信用评分系统为例，分析了不同级别的推理能力是否满足AI法案的定义，指出需要监管明确的地方。研究表明，不仅要考虑单个模型，还要考虑整个数据处理流程，且人类专家的参与会对推理能力产生显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 《欧洲人工智能法案》将“推理能力”作为区分AI系统的关键特征，但未明确定义什么是“推理”，导致某些数据驱动系统（如信用评分系统）是否属于AI监管范围存在灰色地带。作者希望澄清这一概念，为监管提供理论支撑。

Method: 基于统计学习理论，构建推理能力分级框架；结合AI法案及委员会指南，分析各等级是否满足法案中“足够推理能力”的要求；通过构建两个现实的信用评分工作流实例，展示推理发生的位置和方式。

Result: 1) 提出了推理能力的分级框架；2) 识别出哪些级别符合AI法案定义、哪些需要进一步监管澄清；3) 发现推理能力不仅取决于单个模型，还涉及整个数据处理流程；4) 发现人类专家在开发过程中的参与会显著影响系统的推理能力。

Conclusion: 应关注整个数据处理工作流而非仅单个模型来判定AI系统；人类专家参与可能改变系统的推理能力属性；现有AI法案在推理定义上需要更多监管指引。

Abstract: The European AI Act is the first comprehensive regulation of artificial intelligence (AI), setting out extensive obligations, particularly for so-called high-risk and general-purpose AI systems. A key distinguishing feature of AI systems under the AI Act is the capability to infer. Since the AI Act does not clearly define what inference is, there is a gray area for certain data-driven systems. A specific example is credit scoring systems, which are listed by Annex III of the AI Act. At the same time, however, these are often implemented using statistical models for which it is unclear whether they have the capability to infer and thus fall under the AI definition of the AI Act at all.
  Motivated by statistical learning theory, this work develops a framework for grading different levels of the capability to infer.
